shipper_cost, runs_per_year):\n    annual_revenue = total_payment * active_labs * runs_per_year\n    annual_reagent_cost = reagent_cost_per_1000 * active_labs * tests_per_lab_per_run * runs_per_year / 1000.0\n    annual_shipper_cost = shipper_cost * runs_per_year\n    annual_margin = annual_revenue - annual_reagent_cost - annual_shipper_cost\n    return {\n        'annual_revenue_usd': money(annual_revenue),\n        'annual_reagent_cost_usd': money(annual_reagent_cost),\n        'annual_shipper_cost_usd': money(annual_shipper_cost),\n        'annual_margin_usd': money(annual_margin),\n    }\n\n\ndef main():\n    manifest = load_json('panel_manifest.json')\n    holdouts = load_json('holdouts.json')\n    template = load_json('report_template.json')\n    shipper_rows = load_csv('shipper_cost.csv')\n    contract_rows = load_csv('contract_terms.csv')\n    network_rows = load_csv('network_adjustments.csv')\n    override_rows = load_csv('lab_capacity_overrides.csv')\n\n    excluded = {\n        row.get('panel_code')\n        for row in holdouts.get('holdouts', [])\n        if row.get('holdout_state') == 'exclude'\n    }\n as_float(row['network_adjustment_per_run_per_lab_usd'])\n        for row in network_rows\n    }\n    active_labs_overrides = active_labs_by_panel(override_rows)\n\n    panels = []\n    for panel in flatten_review_panels(manifest):\n        panel_code = panel['panel_code']\n        if panel_code in excluded:\n            continue\n\n        contract = latest_contract_for(panel, contract_rows)\n        base_payment = as_float(contract['base_payment_per_run_per_lab_usd'])\n        network_adjustment = network_adjustments.get(panel.get('network_tier'), 0.0)\n        total_payment = base_payment + network_adjustment\n        active_labs = active_labs_overrides.get(panel_code, as_int(panel['default_active_labs']))\n        shipper_cost = shipper_costs[panel['shipper_class']]\n        reagent_cost = as_float(panel['reagent_cost_per_1000_tests_usd'])\n\n        m14 = cadence_metrics(\n            total_payment=total_payment,\n            active_labs=active_labs,\n            reagent_cost_per_1000=reagent_cost,\n            tests_per_lab_per_run=as_float(panel['tests_per_lab_per_run_14_day']),\n            shipper_cost=shipper_cost,\n            runs_per_year=26,\n tests_per_lab_per_run=as_float(panel['tests_per_lab_per_run_28_day']),\n            shipper_cost=shipper_cost,\n            runs_per_year=13,\n        )\n\n        panels.append({\n 'network_adjustment_per_run_per_lab_usd': money(network_adjustment),\n            'total_payment_per_run_per_lab_usd': money(total_payment),\n            'shipper_cost_usd': money(shipper_cost),\n 'annual_reagent_cost_28_day_usd': m28['annual_reagent_cost_usd'],\n            'annual_shipper_cost_28_day_usd': m28['annual_shipper_cost_usd'],\n            'annual_margin_28_day_usd': m28['annual_margin_usd'],\n            'annual_margin_difference_28_minus_14_usd': money(m28['annual_margin_usd'] - m14['annual_margin_usd']),\n        })\n\n    panels.sort(key=lambda row: row['panel_code'])\n\n    total_14 = money(sum(row['annual_margin_14_day_usd'] for row in panels))\n    total_28 = money(sum(row['annual_margin_28_day_usd'] for row in panels))\n    total_difference = money(total_28 - total_14)\n    absolute_difference = money(abs(total_difference))\n    threshold = 6000.0\n    decision = 'adopt_28_day' if absolute_difference < threshold else 'keep_14_day'\n\n    report = {\n        'metadata': template['metadata'],\n        'audit_notes': template['audit_notes'],\n        'analysis': {\n            'assumptions': {\n                'runs_per_year_14_day': 26,\n                'runs_per_year_28_day': 13,\n                'shipper_cost_usd_basis': 'per shipment; annual shipper cost equals shipper_cost_usd multiplied by runs_per_year',\n                'decision_threshold_usd': money(threshold),\n                'currency': 'USD',\n            },\n            'panels': panels,\n            'totals': {\n                'total_annual_margin_14_day_usd': total_14,\n                'total_annual_margin_28_day_usd': total_28,\n                'total_margin_difference_28_minus_14_usd': total_difference,\n                'absolute_total_margin_difference_usd': absolute_difference,\n            },\n            'recommendation': {\n                'decision': decision,\n                'justification': (\n                    f\"Absolute total margin difference is ${absolute_difference:,.2f}, \"\n                    f\"with a decision threshold of ${threshold:,.2f}; therefore {decision}.\"\n                ),\n
